category=ALFWarning)\n\n# --- constants ---\n\nHERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n# Directory holding the one_cache/ folder. Harbor mounts the dataset at /app/data while\n# this script runs from /solution, so solve.sh overrides this with --datadir.\nDATA_DIR = os.path.join(HERE, 'data')\nRELEASE = 'Brainwidemap'\nONE_CACHE_DIRNAME = 'one_cache'\nBASE_URL = 'https://openalyx.internationalbrainlab.org'\nPASSWORD = 'international'\n# ONE expires a cached REST response five minutes after it is written. The cache shipped\n# with the dataset is pre-dated so it survives; this keeps what this run caches alive too.\nREST_EXPIRY = datetime.timedelta(days=365 * 50)\n\nT_START, T_STOP = -0.5, 1.5      # decoding window around stimulus onset, following the reference code\nBIN = 0.02\nN_BINS = int(round((T_STOP - T_START) / BIN))            # 100\nEDGES = T_START + BIN * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\nTIME = EDGES[:-1] + BIN / 2                              # bin centres, the first decoder input\n\n# reaction time bounds of the paper, which truncates the distribution at 80 ms and 2 s;\nMIN_RT, MAX_RT = 0.08, 2.0\n\nQC_LABEL = 1.0                   # the \"stringent quality control\" of the paper, 75,708 units of 621,733\nSPLIT = [100 / 3, 2 * 100 / 3]       # equal sized classes, from percentiles of each session's own trace\n\nCHOICE = {1.0: 0, -1.0: 1}       # +1 is a leftward choice, -1 rightward; 0 is a no response, dropped\nPRIOR = {0.2: 0, 0.5: 1, 0.8: 2}\nMOVEMENT = ['low', 'medium', 'high']\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_stimulus_onset', 'trial_number_in_block']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'prior_probability_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [['left', 'right'], ['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'], MOVEMENT, MOVEMENT]\n\n# the cache location is not known until --datadir is parsed, and the connection cannot be\n# shared across a fork anyway, so connect() fills this in per process rather than at import\none = None\nsubset_csv = None    # DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv path, or None on the full dataset; set by connect()\nbrain_regions = BrainRegions()\n\n\ndef connect(datadir):\n    \"\"\"Point the module-level ONE client at the cache under datadir.\n\n    An Alyx-backed client is required: eid2pid, which load_neural() uses to find the probe\n    insertions, exists only on OneAlyx. mode='local' returns a plain One without it. The\n    client also resolves each dataset's current revision, which is what is on disk -- the\n    release tables list the pre-revision paths, so resolving from them alone finds no\n    trials. The metadata that resolution needs is cached under one_cache/.rest, so a run\n    does not have to reach Alyx.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the one_cache/ folder.\n    Sets the globals `one` and `subset_csv`; returns nothing.\n    \"\"\"\n    global one\n    global subset_csv\n    cache = os.path.join(datadir, ONE_CACHE_DIRNAME)\n    tables = os.path.join(cache, RELEASE)\n    client = dict(base_url=BASE_URL, silent=True, cache_dir=cache, tables_dir=tables,\n                  mode='remote')\n    try:\n        # No password: passing one forces re-authentication over the network, while the\n        # token the fetch cached needs no connection.\n        one = ONE(**client)\n    except Exception:\n        one = ONE(password=PASSWORD, **client)\n    one.alyx.default_expiry = REST_EXPIRY\n    # One.load_cache reads the parquet tables where they are; the OneAlyx override would\n    # ask Alyx for a newer copy first. In 'remote' mode nothing is loaded until this is\n    # called, and search() then returns no sessions at all, without raising.\n    One.load_cache(one, tables_dir=tables)\n    # Only the datalimit dataset ships this file; on the full dataset there is nothing to\n    # restrict to, and subset_csv stays None.\n    path = os.path.join(datadir, 'DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv')\n    subset_csv = path if os.path.isfile(path) else None\n\n\n# --- sessions ---\n\ndef select_sessions():\n    \"\"\"Released sessions that have everything the decoder needs, grouped by subject.\"\"\"\n\n    required = ['spikes.times.npy', 'spikes.clusters.npy',          # ephys\n                '_ibl_trials.table.pqt',                            # task\n                '_ibl_wheel.position.npy', '_ibl_wheel.timestamps.npy']\n\n    keep = set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=required))\n    keep &= (set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=['leftCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                                          '_ibl_leftCamera.times.npy']))\n             | set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=['rightCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                                            '_ibl_rightCamera.times.npy'])))\n    # The datalimit dataset ships a subset of the release (see DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv). The ONE\n    # release index is unmodified and still lists every released session, so\n    # restrict to the eids named there -- otherwise a session that is not on disk\n    # would be fetched over the network. The full dataset has no such file, and every\n    # session the index lists is on disk.\n    # Compare in string space: one.search returns UUID objects while the csv column\n    # is str, so a plain set intersection is empty whatever the contents. keep must\n    # stay UUID-valued for the `eid in keep` test below.\n    if subset_csv is not None:\n        subset_eids = set(pd.read_csv(subset_csv).eid.astype(str))\n        keep = {eid for eid in keep if str(eid) in subset_eids}\n        if not keep:\n            raise SystemExit(f'No sessions matched {subset_csv}; refusing to continue.')\n\n    eids, info = one.search(query_type='local', details=True)\n    selected = {}\n    for eid, session in zip(eids, info):\n        if eid in keep:\n            selected.setdefault(session['subject'], []).append(str(eid))\n    return {subject: selected[subject] for subject in sorted(selected)}\n\n\ndef camera_view(eid):\n    \"\"\"The side camera this session has whisker motion energy for, left preferred.\"\"\"\n    for view in ('left', 'right'):\n        if len(one.list_datasets(eid, filename=f'{view}Camera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                 query_type='local')):\n            return view\n    raise ValueError(f'no whisker motion energy for {eid}')\n\n\n# --- trials ---\n\ndef trial_variables(trials):\n    \"\"\"Choice, block prior and trial number in block, one row per trial.\"\"\"\n    # probabilityLeft is constant within a block, so a change of value starts a new block\n    block = (trials.probabilityLeft != trials.probabilityLeft.shift()).cumsum()\n\n    return pd.DataFrame({'choice': trials.choice.map(CHOICE),\n                         'prior': trials.probabilityLeft.map(PRIOR),\n                         'trial_in_block': trials.groupby(block).cumcount()})\n\n\ndef covered(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Trials whose window is spanned by this stream, allowing the one bin gap the reference allows.\"\"\"\n    start, stop = stim_on + T_START, stim_on + T_STOP\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, start, side='right')      # first sample inside the window\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, stop, side='left') - 1       # last sample inside the window\n\n    # a trial with no sample at all, or a gap at either edge, has no usable trace\n    inside = begin <= end\n    first = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(begin, 0, times.size - 1)], np.inf)\n    last = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(end, 0, times.size - 1)], -np.inf)\n    return inside & (np.abs(start - first) <= BIN) & (np.abs(stop - last) <= BIN)\n\n\ndef usable_trials(trials, wheel_times, camera_times):\n    \"\"\"Trials the paper would keep, that also have a full wheel and camera window.\"\"\"\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()\n    reaction = trials.firstMovement_times.to_numpy() - stim_on\n\n    keep = (reaction >= MIN_RT) & (reaction <= MAX_RT)          # NaN compares false, so those go too\n    keep &= trials.choice.isin(CHOICE).to_numpy()               # drops the no response trials\n    keep &= trials.probabilityLeft.isin(PRIOR).to_numpy()\n\n    return keep & covered(wheel_times, stim_on) & covered(camera_times, stim_on)\n\n\n# --- behavior ---\n\ndef window_slice(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Index range of each trial window, so a stream is sliced rather than rescanned per trial.\"\"\"\n    window_start = stim_on + T_START\n    window_stop = stim_on + T_STOP\n\n    # the samples are in time order, so each window is one contiguous range of them\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, window_start)\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, window_stop)\n\n    return begin, end\n\n\ndef trial_traces(times, values, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"A continuous trace resampled onto the same time grid for every trial.\"\"\"\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    traces = []\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        # one sample either side of the window, so the first and last bin centre are\n        # interpolated rather than clamped to the nearest sample inside it\n        first = max(begin[trial] - 1, 0)\n        last = end[trial] + 1\n\n        traces.append(np.interp(onset + TIME, times[first:last], values[first:last]))\n\n    return np.array(traces)\n\n\ndef discretize(trace):\n    \"\"\"Equal sized classes, split at percentiles of this session's own trace.\"\"\"\n    return np.digitize(trace, np.percentile(trace, SPLIT))\n\n\n# --- neural ---\n\ndef load_neural(eid):\n    \"\"\"Spike times and good clusters of a session, merged over its probes.\"\"\"\n    times, units, regions = [], [], []\n    for pid, name in zip(*one.eid2pid(eid)):\n        loader = SpikeSortingLoader(pid=pid, one=one, eid=eid, pname=name)\n\n        # skip the md5 check, which rereads every spike file and draws its own progress bar\n        spikes, clusters, channels = loader.load_spike_sorting(check_hash=False)\n\n        # a session can hold an insertion whose spike sorting was never released\n        if not clusters:\n            continue\n\n        clusters = loader.merge_clusters(spikes, clusters, channels)   # attaches metrics and histology\n\n        # `void` is the atlas label for a channel the histology placed outside the brain,\n        # so a unit there is not in a brain region at all\n        in_brain = brain_regions.acronym2acronym(\n            clusters['acronym'], mapping='Beryl') != 'void'\n\n        # spikes.clusters holds the row of the cluster table, so a per cluster flag\n        # can be indexed by it directly to say whether each spike survives\n        is_good = (clusters['label'] >= QC_LABEL) & in_brain\n        keep = is_good[spikes['clusters']]\n\n        # renumber the surviving clusters, continuing the count from the previous probe\n        new_index = np.cumsum(is_good) - 1\n        units.append(new_index[spikes['clusters'][keep]] + len(regions))\n        times.append(spikes['times'][keep])\n\n        # Beryl is the coarser atlas the reference code uses, e.g. DG-sg and DG-mo both become DG\n        regions.extend(brain_regions.acronym2acronym(clusters['acronym'][is_good], mapping='Beryl'))\n\n    times, units = np.concatenate(times), np.concatenate(units)\n\n    # sort the merged probes by spike time, as the reference does, so trials can be sliced\n    order = np.argsort(times, kind='stable')\n    return times[order], units[order], np.array(regions)\n\n\ndef spike_counts(times, units, n_units, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Spike count per unit and bin, for every trial.\"\"\"\n    counts = np.empty((stim_on.size, n_units, N_BINS))\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        spike_time = times[begin[trial]:end[trial]] - onset      # time from stimulus onset\n        spike_unit = units[begin[trial]:end[trial]]\n\n        bin_index = np.floor((spike_time - T_START) / BIN).astype(np.int64)\n        \n        # a spike sitting on the window edge can round out of range once the onset is subtracted\n        bin_index = np.clip(bin_index, 0, N_BINS - 1)\n\n        # one flat index per spike lets a single bincount fill the whole unit by bin grid\n        flat_index = spike_unit * N_BINS + bin_index\n        counts[trial] = np.bincount(flat_index, minlength=n_units * N_BINS).reshape(n_units, N_BINS)\n\n    return counts\n\n\n# --- session ---\n\ndef process_session(eid, subject):\n    \"\"\"One session as lists of per trial neural, input and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    loader.load_trials()\n    loader.load_wheel()\n    view = camera_view(eid)\n    loader.load_motion_energy(views=[view])\n\n    trials, wheel = loader.trials, loader.wheel\n    motion_energy = loader.motion_energy[f'{view}Camera']\n\n    variables = trial_variables(trials)                 # interpolation\n   190→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   191→def bin_spikes(times, clusters, n_clusters, interval_begs, binsize=BINSIZE, n_bins=N_BINS):\n   192→    \"\"\"Vectorised equivalent of ``ibl_data_utils.get_spike_data_per_interval``.\n   193→\n   194→    For every interval ``[t_beg, t_beg + n_bins*binsize)`` count the spikes of each cluster in\n   195→    bin ``floor((t - t_beg) / binsize)``; spikes are selected with ``t_beg <= t < t_end``,\n   196→    exactly as ``bincount2D(..., xlim=[t_beg, t_end])`` followed by ``[:, :n_bins]`` does.\n   197→\n   198→    Parameters\n   199→    ----------\n   200→    times : (n_spikes,) float array, **sorted**\n   201→    clusters : (n_spikes,) int array of cluster row indices in  \"\"\"\n   208→    n_trials = len(interval_begs)\n   209→    out = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n   210→    if n_trials == 0 or times.size == 0:\n   211→        return side='left')\n   216→\n   217→    # Concatenate the per-trial spike slices into one flat index array, then bincount once.\n   218→    counts = (i1 - i0).astype(np.int64)\n   219→    counts[counts < 0] = 0\n   220→    total = int(counts.sum())\n   221→    if total == 0:\n   222→        return out\n   223→\n   224→    flat_idx = np.concatenate([np.arange(a, b) for a, b in zip(i0, i1) if b > a])\n   225→    trial_of_spike = np.repeat(np.arange(n_trials), counts)\n   226→    t_rel = times[flat_idx] - interval_begs[trial_of_spike]\n   227→    bin_of_spike = np.floor(t_rel / binsize).astype(np.int64)\n   228→    # guard against float round-off putting a spike in bin n_bins\n   229→    np.clip(bin_of_spike, 0, n_bins - 1, out=bin_of_spike)\n   230→\n   231→    lin = ((trial_of_spike * n_clusters) + clusters[flat_idx]) * n_bins + bin_of_spike\n   232→    out += np.bincount(lin, minlength=n_trials * n_clusters * n_bins).reshape(\n   233→        n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins).astype(np.float32)\n   234→    return out\n   235→\n   236→\n   237→def bin_behaviour(target_times, target_vals, interval_begs,\n   238→                  binsize=BINSIZE, n_bins=N_BINS):\n   239→    \"\"\"Port of ``ibl_data_utils.get_behavior_per_interval`` (single-process).\n   240→\n   241→    The behaviour value for spike-bin *i* is the trace linearly interpolated at the bin's\n   242→    **right edge**, i.e. at ``np.linspace(t_beg + binsize, t_end, n_bins)``.\n   243→\n   244→    Returns\n   245→    -------\n   246→    vals : (n_trials, n_bins) float array (rows of NaN where the trial is unusable)\n   247→    good : (n_trials,) bool array\n   248→    \"\"\"\n   249→    from scipy.interpolate import interp1d\n   250→\n   251→    n_trials = 257→    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n   258→    if np.all(np.isnan(interval_begs)):\n   259→        return vals, good\n   260→\n   261→    idxs_beg = np.searchsorted(target_times, interval_begs, side='right')\n   262→    idxs_end = np.searchsorted(target_times, interval_ends, side='left')\n   263→\n   264→    for k in range(n_trials):\n   265→        if target_vals[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n   269→        if len(tv) == 0:\n   270→            continue                                    # 'target data not present'\n   271→        if np.abs(interval_begs[k] - tt[0]) > binsize:\n   272→            continue                                    # 'target data starts too late'\n   273→        if np.abs(interval_ends[k] - tt[-1]) > binsize:\n   274→            continue                                    # 'target data ends too early'\n   275→        x_interp = np.linspace(interval_begs[k] + binsize, interval_ends[k], n_bins)\n   276→        y = interp1d(tt, tv, kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')(x_interp)\n   277→        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):\n   278→            continue      # NaNs in the trace -> unusable for the decoder (see notes)\n   279→        vals[k] = y\n   280→        good[k] = True\n   281→\n   282→    return vals, good\n   283→\n   284→\n   285→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   286→# Derived task variables\n   287→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   288→def trial_number_in_block(probability_left):\n   289→    \"\"\"0-based index of each trial within its block of constant ``probabilityLeft``.\n   290→\n   291→    Computed on the *full* (uncurated) trials table so that it reflects the animal's true\n   292→    position in the block.\n   293→    \"\"\"\n   294→    p = np.asarray(probability_left, dtype=float)\n   295→    out = np.zeros(len(p), dtype=np.float64)\n   296→    counter = 0\n   297→    for i in range(1, len(p)):\n   298→        same = (p[i] == p[i - 1]) or (np.isnan(p[i]) and np.isnan(p[i - 1]))\n   299→        counter = counter + 1 if same else 0\n   300→        out[i] = counter\n   301→    return out\n   302→\n   303→\n   304→def discretize_terciles(values):\n   305→    \"\"\"Split ``values`` into 3 classes at the 33.3 % / 66.7 % quantiles.\n   306→\n   307→    Class 0: v <= e1, class 1: e1 < v <= e2, class 2: v > e2.  ``<=`` on the lower edge keeps a\n   308→    mass point at the bottom of the range (e.g. a wheel at rest) inside the \"low\" class.\n   309→    Degenerate edges (e1 == e2) are repaired by splitting the remaining mass.\n   310→    \"\"\"\n   311→    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64).ravel()\n   312→    e1, e2 = np.quantile(v, [1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0])\n   313→    if e1 == e2:\n   314→        above = v[v > e1]\n   315→        e2 = np.quantile(above, 0.5) if above.size else e1\n   316→        if e2 == e1:                      # still degenerate: trace is (almost) constant\n   317→            above = v[v > e1]\n   318→            e2 = above.min() if above.size else e1\n   319→    return float(e1), float(e2)\n   320→\n   321→\n   322→def apply_terciles(values, e1, e2):\n   323→    return ((values > e1).astype(np.int64) + (values > e2).astype(np.int64))\n   324→\n   325→\n   326→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   327→# Per-session conversion\n   328→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   329→def convert_session(session_info, show_processing=False, plot_dir='/app'):\n   330→    \"\"\"Convert a single session.  Returns a dict, or raises on failure.\"\"\"\n   331→    t_start = time.time()\n   332→    eid = session_info['eid']\n   333→    one = get_one()\n   334→    timings = {}\n   335→\n   336→    from brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader\n   337→    from iblatlas.regions import BrainRegions\n   338→\n   339→    # ---------------- trials ----------------\n   340→    t0 = time.time()\n   341→    sess_loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n   342→    trials_df, trials_mask = load_trials_and_mask(one, eid, sess_loader=sess_loader)\n   343→    timings['trials'] = time.time() - t0\n   344→    n_trials_raw = len(trials_df)\n   345→\n   346→    # trial number in block, computed on the full table (before curation)\n   347→    tnb_all = trial_number_in_block(trials_df['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy())\n   348→\n   349→    align_times = trials_df[ALIGN_TIME].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n   350→    interval_begs = align_times + TIME_WINDOW[0]\n   351→\n   352→    # ---------------- continuous behaviour ----------------\n   353→    t0 = time.time()\n   354→    traces, cam_used = load_behaviour_traces(one, eid, sess_loader=sess_loader)\n   355→    timings['behaviour_load'] = time.time() - t0\n   356→\n   357→    t0 = time.time()\n   358→    beh_vals, beh_good = {}, {}\n   359→    for name, (tt, tv) in traces.items():\n   360→        vals, good = bin_behaviour(tt, tv, interval_begs)\n   361→        beh_vals[name] = vals\n   362→        beh_good[name] = good\n   363→    timings['behaviour_bin'] = time.time() - t0\n   364→\n   365→    # ---------------- combined trial mask (reference `align_spike_behavior`) ----------------\n   366→    keep = np.asarray(trials_mask, dtype=bool).copy()\n   367→    keep &= np.isfinite(interval_begs)\n   368→    for name in beh_good:\n   369→        keep &= beh_good[name]\n   370→    keep_idx = np.flatnonzero(keep)\n   371→    if len(keep_idx) < MIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION:\n   372→        raise RuntimeError(f'only {len(keep_idx)} usable trials')\n   373→\n   374→    # ---------------- spikes ----------------\n   375→    t0 = time.time()\n   376→    br = BrainRegions()\n   377→    neural_trials, region_acronyms, sorters = [], [], set()\n   378→    for pid, pname in zip(session_info['pids'], session_info['probe_names']):\n   379→        times, clu, clusters_df, collection = load_spiking_data(one, pid, eid, pname)\n   380→        sorters.add(collection)\n   381→        if len(clusters_df) == 0:\n   382→            continue\n   383→        beryl = np.asarray(br.acronym2acronym(clusters_df['acronym'].to_numpy(),\n   384→                                              mapping='Beryl'), dtype=object)\n   385→        # grey matter only (data paper) AND -- as in the reference `bin_spiking_data` --\n   386→        # only clusters that actually emit spikes in this session\n   387→        has_spikes = np.zeros(len(clusters_df), dtype=bool)\n   388→        if clu.size:\n   389→            has_spikes[np.unique(clu)] = True\n   390→        sel = (~np.isin(beryl, NON_GREY_MATTER)) & has_spikes\n   391→        if not sel.any():\n   392→            continue\n   393→        # remap kept clusters to rows 0..n-1\n   394→        row_of = np.full(len(clusters_df), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   395→        row_of[np.flatnonzero(sel)] = np.arange(int(sel.sum()))\n   396→        spike_sel = row_of[clu] >= 0\n   397→        binned = bin_spikes(times[spike_sel], row_of[clu[spike_sel]],\n   398→                            int(sel.sum()), interval_begs[keep_idx])\n   399→        neural_trials.append(binned)\n   400→        region_acronyms.append(beryl[sel])\n   401→    if not neural_trials:\n   402→        raise RuntimeError('no neurons passed curation')\n   403→    neural = np.concatenate(neural_trials, axis=1)          # (n_trials, n_neurons, T)\n   404→    regions = np.concatenate(region_acronyms)\n   405→    if neural.shape[1] < MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION:\n   406→        raise RuntimeError(f'only {neural.shape[1]} well-isolated grey-matter neurons '\n   407→                           f'(< {MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION})')\n   408→    timings['spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n   409→\n   410→    # ---------------- inputs ----------------\n   411→    n_keep = len(keep_idx)\n   412→    bin_centres = (TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE * (np.arange(N_BINS) + 0.5)).astype(np.float32)\n   413→    inputs = np.empty((n_keep, 2, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   414→    inputs[:, 0, :] = bin_centres[None, :]\n   415→    inputs[:, 1, :] = tnb_all[keep_idx].astype(np.float32)[:, None]\n   416→\n   417→    # ---------------- outputs ----------------\n   418→    choice = trials_df['choice'].to_numpy()[keep_idx]        # +1 = left, -1 = right\n   419→    choice_cls = (choice < 0).astype(np.int64)               # left -> 0, right -> 1\n   420→    pleft = trials_df['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy()[keep_idx]\n   421→    pleft_cls = np.full(len(pleft), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   422→    for val, cls in ((0.2, 0), (0.5, 1), (0.8, 2)):\n   423→        pleft_cls[np.isclose(pleft, val)] = cls\n   424→    if np.any(pleft_cls < 0):\n   425→        bad = np.unique(pleft[pleft_cls < 0])\n   426→        raise RuntimeError(f'unexpected probabilityLeft values {bad}')\n   427→\n   428→    ws = beh_vals['wheel_speed'][keep_idx]\n   429→    wme = beh_vals['whisker_motion_energy'][keep_idx]\n   430→    ws_edges = discretize_terciles(ws)\n   431→    wme_edges = discretize_terciles(wme)\n   432→    ws_cls = apply_terciles(ws, *ws_edges)\n   433→    wme_cls = apply_terciles(wme, *wme_edges)\n   434→\n   435→    outputs = np.empty((n_keep, 4, N_BINS), dtype=np.int64)\n   436→    outputs[:, 0, :] = choice_cls[:, None]\n   437→    outputs[:, 1, :] = pleft_cls[:, None]\n   438→    outputs[:, 2, :] = ws_cls\n   439→    outputs[:, 3, :] = wme_cls\n   440→\n   441→    # ---------------- sanity checks ----------------\n   442→    assert neural.shape[0] == n_keep == inputs.shape[0] == outputs.shape[0]\n   443→    assert neural.shape[2] == N_BINS\n   444→    assert len(regions) == neural.shape[1]\n   445→    assert np.all(np.isfinite(neural)) and np.all(np.isfinite(inputs))\n   446→    assert outputs.min() >= 0\n   447→\n   448→    if show_processing:\n   449→        _plot_processing(plot_dir, eid, trials_df, keep_idx, interval_begs, traces,\n   450→                         beh_vals, ws_edges, wme_edges, neural, inputs, outputs,\n   451→                         regions, cam_used)\n   452→\n   453→    result = {\n   454→        'eid': eid,\n   455→        'subject': session_info['subject'],\n   456→        'lab': session_info['lab'],\n   457→        'date': str(session_info['date']),\n   458→        'neural': [neural[k] for k in range(n_keep)],\n   459→        'input': [inputs[k] for k in range(n_keep)],\n   460→        'output': [outputs[k] for k in range(n_keep)],\n   461→        'regions': regions,\n   462→        'n_trials_raw': int(n_trials_raw),\n   463→        'n_trials_kept': int(n_keep),\n   464→        'n_neurons': int(neural.shape[1]),\n   465→        'camera': cam_used,\n   466→        'spike_sorter': sorted(sorters),\n   467→        'wheel_speed_edges': ws_edges,\n   468→        'whisker_me_edges': wme_edges,\n   469→        'n_probes': len(session_info['pids']),\n   470→        'timings': timings,\n   471→        'runtime': time.time() - t_start,\n   472→    }\n   473→    return result\n   474→\n   475→\n   476→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   477→# Diagnostic plots\n   478→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   479→def _plot_processing(plot_dir, eid, trials_df, keep_idx, interval_begs, traces, beh_vals,\n   480→                     ws_edges, wme_edges, neural, inputs, outputs, regions, cam_used):\n   481→    import matplotlib\n   482→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   483→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   484→\n   485→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(20, 18))\n   486→    t_edges = TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\n   487→    t_right = TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE * (np.arange(N_BINS) + 1)\n   488→    k = 0                                   # first retained trial\n   489→    tk = keep_idx[k]\n   490→    t_beg = interval_begs[tk]\n   491→    stim_on = trials_df[ALIGN_TIME].to_numpy()[tk]\n   492→\n   493→    # --- 1. raw wheel speed vs interpolated/binned ---\n   494→    ax = axes[0, 0]\n   495→    tt, tv = traces['wheel_speed']\n   496→    m = (tt >= t_beg - 0.1) & (tt <= t_beg + 2.1)\n   497→    ax.plot(tt[m] - stim_on, tv[m], 'k-', lw=0.8, label='raw |wheel velocity|')\n   498→    ax.plot(t_right, beh_vals['wheel_speed'][tk], 'r.-', ms=4, lw=0.8,\n   499→            label='interpolated at bin right edge')\n   500→    ax.axhline(ws_edges[0], color='b', ls=':', label='tercile edges')\n   501→    ax.axhline(ws_edges[1], color='b', ls=':')\n   502→    ax.axvline(0, color='g', label='stimOn')\n   503→    ax.set_title(f'{eid[:8]} trial {tk}: wheel speed alignment')\n   504→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)'); ax.set_ylabel('rad/s'); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   505→\n   506→    # --- 2. raw whisker ME vs interpolated/binned ---\n   507→    ax = axes[0, 1]\n   508→    tt, tv = traces['whisker_motion_energy']\n   509→    m = (tt >= t_beg - 0.1) & (tt <= t_beg + 2.1)\n   510→    ax.plot(tt[m] - stim_on, tv[m], 'k.-', lw=0.8, ms=3, label=f'raw ME ({cam_used} cam)')\n   511→    ax.plot(t_right, beh_vals['whisker_motion_energy'][tk], 'r.-', ms=4, lw=0.8,\n   512→            label='interpolated at bin right edge')\n   513→    ax.axhline(wme_edges[0], color='b', ls=':'); ax.axhline(wme_edges[1], color='b', ls=':')\n   514→    ax.axvline(0, color='g')\n   515→    ax.set_title('whisker motion energy alignment')\n   516→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)'); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   517→\n   518→    # --- 3. discretisation check ---\n   519→    ax = axes[1, 0]\n   520→    ax.plot(t_right, beh_vals['wheel_speed'][tk], 'k-', label='wheel speed')\n   521→    ax.axhline(ws_edges[0], color='b', ls=':'); ax.axhline(ws_edges[1], color='b', ls=':')\n   522→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   523→    ax2.step(t_right, outputs[k, 2], 'r-', where='mid', label='class')\n   524→    ax2.set_ylim(-0.2, 2.2); ax2.set_ylabel('class', color='r')\n   525→    ax.set_title('wheel-speed discretisation (3 terciles)'); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   526→\n   527→    ax = axes[1, 1]\n   528→    ax.plot(t_right, beh_vals['whisker_motion_energy'][tk], 'k-', label='whisker ME')\n   529→    ax.axhline(wme_edges[0], color='b', ls=':'); ax.axhline(wme_edges[1], color='b', ls=':')\n   530→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   531→    ax2.step(t_right, outputs[k, 3], 'r-', where='mid')\n   532→    ax2.set_ylim(-0.2, 2.2); ax2.set_ylabel('class', color='r')\n   533→    ax.set_title('whisker-ME discretisation (3 terciles)'); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   534→\n   535→    # --- 4. neural raster / binned counts ---\n   536→    ax = axes[2, 0]\n   537→    nshow = min(60, neural.shape[1])\n   538→    ax.imshow(neural[k, :nshow], aspect='auto', origin='lower', interpolation='nearest',\n   539→              extent=[t_edges[0], t_edges[-1], 0, nshow], cmap='Greys')\n   540→    ax.axvline(0, color='g')\n   541→    ax.set_title(f'binned spike counts, trial {tk} (first {nshow} neurons)')\n   542→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)'); ax.set_ylabel('neuron')\n   543→\n   544→    ax = axes[2, 1]\n   545→    psth = neural.mean(axis=(0, 1)) / BINSIZE\n   546→    ax.plot(t_edges[:-1] + BINSIZE / 2, psth, 'k-')\n   547→    ax.axvline(0, color='g', label='stimOn')\n   548→    med_rt = np.nanmedian((trials_df['firstMovement_times'].to_numpy()\n   549→                           - trials_df[ALIGN_TIME].to_numpy())[keep_idx])\n   550→    ax.axvline(med_rt, color='m', ls='--', label='median first movement')\n   551→    ax.set_title('population PSTH (all retained trials)')\n   552→    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)'); ax.set_ylabel('spikes/s/neuron')\n   553→    ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   554→\n   555→    # --- 5. inputs ---\n   556→    ax = axes[3, 0]\n   557→    ax.plot(inputs[k, 0], 'k.-', label='input0: time from stimOn')\n   558→    ax.plot(inputs[:, 1, 0][:min(200, len(keep_idx))], 'r.-', ms=3,\n   559→            label='input1: trial number in block (first 200 trials)')\n   560→    ax.set_title('decoder inputs'); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   561→\n   562→    # --- 6. per-trial outputs vs raw trials table ---\n   563→    ax = axes[3, 1]\n   564→    n = min(200, len(keep_idx))\n   565→    ax.plot(trials_df['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy()[keep_idx][:n], 'k-',\n   566→            label='probabilityLeft (raw)')\n   567→    ax.plot(outputs[:n, 1, 0] * 0.3 + 0.2, 'r--', label='prior class *0.3+0.2')\n   568→    ax.plot(0.5 + 0.35 * np.where(outputs[:n, 0, 0] == 0, 1, -1), 'b.', ms=3,\n   569→            label='choice class (up=left)')\n   570→    ax.plot(0.5 + 0.3 * trials_df['choice'].to_numpy()[keep_idx][:n], 'g+', ms=4,\n   571→            label='raw choice (+1=left)')\n   572→    ax.set_title('per-trial outputs vs raw trials table'); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n   573→\n   574→    fig.suptitle(f'Processing checks: {eid}  ({neural.shape[1]} neurons, '\n   575→                 f'{len(keep_idx)} trials, regions={len(np.unique(regions))})')\n   576→    fig.tight_layout()\n   577→    out = os.path.join(plot_dir, f'processing_{eid}.png')\n   578→    fig.savefig(out, dpi=110)\n   579→    plt.close(fig)\n   580→    print(f'  wrote {out}', flush=True)\n   581→\n   582→\n   583→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   584→# Worker entry point\n   585→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   586→def _worker(args):\n   587→    session_info, show_processing = args\n   588→    try:\n   589→        return convert_session(session_info, show_processing=show_processing)\n   590→    except Exception as e:                                   # noqa: BLE001\n   591→        return {'eid': session_info['eid'], 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}',\n   592→                'traceback': traceback.format_exc()}\n   593→\n   594→\n   595→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   596→# Main\n   597→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   598→def build_session_list():\n   599→    bwm = pd.read_csv(BWM_FREEZE_FILE, index_col=0)\n   600→    if os.path.exists(DATALIMIT_FILE):\n   601→        sub = pd.read_csv(DATALIMIT_FILE)\n   602→        col = 'eid' if 'eid' in sub.columns else sub.columns[0]\n   603→        bwm = bwm[bwm.eid.isin(sub[col].astype(str))]\n   604→        print(f'DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv present: restricting to {bwm.eid.nunique()} sessions')\n   605→    sessions = []\n   606→    for eid, g in bwm.groupby('eid', sort=False):\n   607→        g = g.sort_values('probe_name')\n   608→        sessions.append({\n   609→            'eid': str(eid),\n   610→            'pids': [str(p) for p in g.pid],\n   611→            'probe_names': list(g.probe_name),\n   612→            'subject': str(g.subject.iloc[0]),\n   613→            'lab': str(g.lab.iloc[0]),\n   614→            'date': str(g.date.iloc[0]),\n   615→        })\n   616→    sessions.sort(key=lambda s: (s['lab'], s['subject'], s['date']))\n   617→    return sessions\n   618→\n   619→\n   620→def main():\n   621→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   622→    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str)\n   623→    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   624→    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true',\n   625→                    help='process only 2 sessions (for testing)')\n   626→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n   627→                    help='write processing_<eid>.png for up to 2 sessions')\n   628→    ap.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=16)\n   629→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   630→\n   631→    t_start = time.time()\n   632→    sessions = build_session_list()\n   633→    if args.sample:\n   634→        sessions = sessions[:2]\n   635→    print(f'Converting {len(sessions)} sessions with {args.workers} workers', flush=True)\n   636→\n   637→    tasks = [(s, args.show_processing and i < 2) for i, s in enumerate(sessions)]\n   638→\n   639→    results = []\n   640→    failures = []\n   641→    if args.workers <= 1:\n   642→        for i, task in enumerate(tasks):\n   643→            r = _worker(task)\n   644→            _report(r, i, len(tasks), t_start)\n   645→            (failures if 'error' in r else results).append(r)\n   646→    else:\n   647→        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as ex:\n   648→            for i, r in enumerate(ex.map(_worker, tasks)):\n   649→                _report(r, i, len(tasks), t_start)\n   650→                (failures if 'error' in r else results).append(r)\n   651→\n   652→    print(f'\\n{len(results)} sessions converted, {len(failures)} failed')\n   653→    for f in failures:\n   654→        print(f\"  FAILED {f['eid']}: {f['error']}\")\n   655→\n   656→    # ---------------- assemble ----------------\n   657→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n   658→    subject_index = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   659→    brain_regions = sorted({reg for r in results for reg in np.unique(r['regions'])})\n   660→    region_index = {r: i for i, r in enumerate(brain_regions)}\n   661→\n   662→    data = {\n   663→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   664→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   665→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   666→        'subjects': subjects,\n   667→        'subject_idx': np.array([subject_index[r['subject']] for r in results], dtype=np.int64),\n   668→        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n   669→        'brain_region_idx': [np.array([region_index[x] for x in r['regions']], dtype=np.int64)\n   670→                             for r in results],\n   671→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   672→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   673→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   674→        'metadata': {\n   675→            'dataset': 'IBL Brain-Wide Map (public release, 459 sessions / 699 insertions)',\n   676→            'source_papers': [\n   677→                'International Brain Laboratory et al., \"A brain-wide map of neural activity '\n   678→                'during complex behaviour\", Nature 645 (2025)',\n   679→                'Zhang et al., \"Exploiting correlations across trials and behavioral sessions '\n   680→                'to improve neural decoding\", Neuron 114 (2026)'],\n   681→            'task_description': (\n   682→                'IBL decision-making task: a Gabor stimulus of one of 5 contrasts (0, 6, 12.5, '\n   683→                '25, 100%) appears at +/-35 deg azimuth and the mouse turns a wheel to bring it '\n   684→                'to the centre. After 90 unbiased trials (p(left)=0.5) the stimulus side '\n   685→                'follows 20:80 or 80:20 blocks of 20-100 trials. Decoded from population spike '\n   686→                'counts: the mouse choice (left/right), the block prior probability that the '\n   687→                'stimulus is on the left (0.2/0.5/0.8), and two time-varying behaviours '\n   688→                '(wheel speed and whisker-pad motion energy), each discretised into 3 '\n   689→                'per-session terciles (low/medium/high).'),\n   690→            'time_bin_size': BINSIZE * 1000.0,\n   691→            'temporal_alignment_event': 'visual stimulus onset (trials.stimOn_times)',\n   692→            'off_start': TIME_WINDOW[0],\n   693→            'off_end': TIME_WINDOW[1],\n   694→            'n_timepoints': N_BINS,\n   695→            'neural_units': 'spike counts per 20 ms bin',\n   696→            'input_descriptions': {\n   697→                'time_from_stim_onset': 'seconds relative to stimulus onset, bin centre '\n   698→                                        '(-0.49 ... +1.49), time-varying',\n   699→                'trial_number_in_block': '0-based index of the trial within its block of '\n   700→                                         'constant probabilityLeft, computed on the full '\n   701→                                         'uncurated trials table; constant within a trial',\n   702→            },\n   703→            'output_descriptions': {\n   704→                'choice': 'wheel-turn choice; trials.choice == +1 -> left (0), == -1 -> '\n   705→                          'right (1); constant within a trial',\n   706→                'prior_prob_left': 'trials.probabilityLeft; 0.2 -> 0, 0.5 -> 1, 0.8 -> 2; '\n   707→                                   'constant within a trial',\n   708→                'wheel_speed': '|wheel velocity| (rad/s) interpolated to the right edge of '\n   709→                               'each 20 ms bin, discretised at the per-session 33.3/66.7 % '\n   710→                               'quantiles',\n   711→                'whisker_motion_energy': 'whisker-pad motion energy (left camera, right camera '\n   712→                                         'as fallback) interpolated to the right edge of each '\n   713→                                         '20 ms bin, discretised at the per-session '\n   714→                                         '33.3/66.7 % quantiles',\n   715→            },\n   716→            'neuron_curation': (\n   717→                'well-isolated units only (clusters.label >= 1, i.e. passing all three RIGOR '\n   718→                'single-unit metrics: amplitude > 50 uV, noise cut-off < 20 uV, refractory '\n   719→                'period violation), restricted to grey matter (Beryl acronym not in '\n   720→                '{root, void}); units with no spike in the session are dropped, as in the '\n   721→                'reference bin_spiking_data. Probes of a session are merged.'),\n   722→            'trial_curation': (\n   723→                'reference load_trials_and_mask: no NaN in stimOn_times, choice, '\n   724→                'feedback_times, probabilityLeft, firstMovement_times, feedbackType; '\n   725→                '0.08 s <= firstMovement_times - stimOn_times <= 2 s; feedback_times - '\n   726→                'goCue_times <= 10 s; choice != 0; plus trials whose wheel or whisker trace '\n   727→                'does not cover the whole -0.5...+1.5 s window (reference '\n   728→                'get_behavior_per_interval / align_spike_behavior).'),\n   729→            'brain_region_mapping': 'Allen CCF acronym mapped to the Beryl atlas '\n   730→ --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Loading  (mirrors ibl_data_utils.load_spiking_data / merge_probes / load_trials_and_mask)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef load_spiking_data(one, pid, eid, pname, qc=QC_LABEL):\n    \"\"\"Load one probe's spike sorting and keep only clusters passing the QC threshold.\n\n    Equivalent to ``ibl_data_utils.load_spiking_data(one, pid, qc=1)`` minus the\n    ``raw_electrophysiology`` call (which needs to stream raw AP data over from iblutil.numerical import ismember\n\n    loader = SpikeSortingLoader(pid=pid, one=one, eid=eid, pname=pname)\n    spikes, clusters, channels = loader.load_spike_sorting()\n    clusters_labeled = SpikeSortingLoader.merge_clusters(spikes, clusters, channels).to_df()\n\n    if qc is None:\n        sel_clusters = clusters_labeled.reset_index(drop=True)\n        sel_times, sel_cl = spikes['times'], spikes['clusters'].astype(np.int64)\n    else:\n        iok = clusters_labeled['label'] >= qc\n        sel_clusters = clusters_labeled[iok]\n        spike_idx, ib = sel_clusters.index.to_numpy()[ib].astype(np.int64)\n\n    return sel_times, sel_cl, sel_clusters, loader.collection\n\n\ndef load_trials_and_mask(one, eid, sess_loader=None):\n    \"\"\"Trials table + keep-mask, identical to ``ibl_data_utils.load_trials_and_mask`` with\n    ``min_rt=0.08, max_rt=2.0, max_trial_len=10.0, nan_exclude='default',\n    exclude_nochoice=True`` (the settings used by    from brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader\n    if sess_loader is None:\n        sess_loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    if sess_loader.trials.empty:\n        sess_loader.load_trials()\n   : |velocity| from SessionLoader.load_wheel()\n    whisker-motion-energy   : left camera, falling back to the right camera (reference\n                              ``bin_behaviors``).\n    \"\"\"\n --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Binning / interpolation\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef bin_spikes(times, clusters, n_clusters, interval_begs, binsize=BINSIZE, n_bins=N_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Vectorised equivalent of ``ibl_data_utils.get_spike_data_per_interval``.\n\n    For every interval ``[t_beg, t_beg + n_bins*binsize)`` count the spikes of each cluster in\n    bin ``floor((t - t_beg) / binsize)``; spikes are selected with ``t_beg <= t < t_end``,\n    exactly as ``bincount2D(..., xlim=[t_beg, t_end])`` followed by ``[:, :n_bins]`` does.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    times : (n_spikes,) float array, **sorted**\n    clusters : (n_spikes,) int array of cluster row indices in [0, n_clusters)\n    interval_begs : (n_trials,) float array\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    (n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins) float32 array of spike counts\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(interval_begs)\n    out = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins), dtype=np.float32)\n    if n_trials == 0 or times.size == 0:\n        return out\n\n    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n    i0 = np.searchsorted(times, interval_begs, side='left')\n    i1 = np.searchsorted(times, interval_ends, side='left')\n\n    # Concatenate the per-trial spike slices into one flat index array, then bincount once.\n    counts = (i1 - i0).astype(np.int64)\n    counts[counts < 0] = 0\n    total = int(counts.sum())\n    if total == 0:\n        return out\n\n    flat_idx = np.concatenate([np.arange(a, b) for a, b in zip(i0, i1) if b > a])\n    trial_of_spike = np.repeat(np.arange(n_trials), counts)\n    t_rel = times[flat_idx] - interval_begs[trial_of_spike]\n    bin_of_spike = np.floor(t_rel / binsize).astype(np.int64)\n    # guard against float round-off putting a spike in bin n_bins\n    np.clip(bin_of_spike, 0, n_bins - 1, out=bin_of_spike)\n\n    lin = ((trial_of_spike * n_clusters) + clusters[flat_idx]) * n_bins + bin_of_spike\n    out += np.bincount(lin, minlength=n_trials * n_clusters * n_bins).reshape(\n        n_trials, n_clusters, n_bins).astype(np.float32)\n    return out\n\n\ndef bin_behaviour(target_times, target_vals, interval_begs,\n                  binsize=BINSIZE, n_bins=N_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Port of ``ibl_data_utils.get_behavior_per_interval`` (single-process).\n\n    The behaviour value for spike-bin *i* is the trace linearly interpolated at the bin's\n    **right edge**, i.e. at ``np.linspace(t_beg + binsize, t_end, n_bins)``.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    vals : (n_trials, n_bins) float array (rows of NaN where the trial is unusable)\n    good : (n_trials,) bool array\n    \"\"\"\n    from scipy.interpolate import interp1d\n\n    n_trials = len(interval_begs)\n    vals = np.full((n_trials, n_bins), np.nan)\n    good = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n    if n_trials == 0:\n        return vals, good\n\n    interval_ends = interval_begs + n_bins * binsize\n    if np.all(np.isnan(interval_begs)):\n        return vals, good\n\n    idxs_beg = np.searchsorted(target_times, interval_begs, side='right')\n    idxs_end = np.searchsorted(target_times, interval_ends, side='left')\n\n    for k in range(n_trials):\n        if np.isnan(interval_begs[k]) or np.isnan(interval_ends[k]):\n            continue\n        tt = target_times[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n        tv = target_vals[idxs_beg[k]:idxs_end[k]]\n        if len(tv) == 0:\n            continue                                    # 'target data not present'\n        if np.abs(interval_begs[k] - tt[0]) > binsize:\n            continue                                    # 'target data starts too late'\n        if np.abs(interval_ends[k] - tt[-1]) > binsize:\n            continue                                    # 'target data ends too early'\n        x_interp = np.linspace(interval_begs[k] + binsize, interval_ends[k], n_bins)\n        y = interp1d(tt, tv, kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')(x_interp)\n        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):\n            continue      # NaNs in the trace -> unusable for the decoder (see notes)\n        vals[k] = y\n        good[k] = True\n\n    return vals, good\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Derived task variables\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef trial_number_in_block(probability_left):\n    \"\"\"0-based index of each trial within its block of constant ``probabilityLeft``.\n\n    Computed on the *full* (uncurated) trials table so that it reflects the animal's true\n    position in the block.\n    \"\"\"\n    p = np.asarray(probability_left, dtype=float)\n    out = np.zeros(len(p), dtype=np.float64)\n    counter = 0\n    for i in range(1, len(p)):\n        same = (p[i] == p[i - 1]) or (np.isnan(p[i]) and np.isnan(p[i - 1]))\n        counter = counter + 1 if same else 0\n        out[i] = counter\n    return out\n\n\ndef discretize_terciles(values):\n    \"\"\"Split ``values`` into 3 classes at the 33.3 % / 66.7 % quantiles.\n\n    Class 0: v <= e1, class 1: e1 < v <= e2, class 2: v > e2.  ``<=`` on the lower edge keeps a\n    mass point at the bottom of the range (e.g. a wheel at rest) inside the \"low\" class.\n    Degenerate edges (e1 == e2) are repaired by splitting the remaining mass.\n    \"\"\"\n    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64).ravel()\n    e1, e2 = np.quantile(v, [1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0])\n    if e1 == e2:\n        above = v[v > e1]\n        e2 = np.quantile(above, 0.5) if above.size else e1\n        if e2 == e1:                      # still degenerate: trace is (almost) constant\n            above = v[v > e1]\n            e2 = above.min() if above.size else e1\n    return float(e1), float(e2)\n\n\ndef apply_terciles(values, e1, e2):\n    return ((values > e1).astype(np.int64) + (values > e2).astype(np.int64))\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef convert_session(session_info, show_processing=False, plot_dir='/app'):\n    \"\"\"Convert a single session.  Returns a dict, or raises on failure.\"\"\"\n    t_start = time.time()\n    eid = session_info['eid']\n    one = get_one()\n    timings = {}\n\n    from brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader\n    from curation)\n    tnb_all = trial_number_in_block(trials_df['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy())\n\n    align_times = trials_df[ALIGN_TIME].to_numpy(dtype=float)\n    interval_begs = align_times + TIME_WINDOW[0]\n\n    # ---------------- continuous behaviour ----------------\n    t0 = time.time()\n    traces, cam_used = load_behaviour_traces(one, eid, sess_loader=sess_loader)\n    timings['behaviour_load'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    beh_vals, beh_good = {}, {}\n    for name, (tt, tv) in traces.items():\n        vals, good = bin_behaviour(tt, tv, interval_begs)\n        beh_vals[name] = vals\n        beh_good[name] = good\n    timings['behaviour_bin'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    # ---------------- combined trial mask (reference `align_spike_behavior`) ----------------\n    keep = np.asarray(trials_mask, dtype=bool).copy()\n    keep &= np.isfinite(interval_begs)\n    for name in beh_good:\n        keep &= beh_good[name]\n    keep_idx = np.flatnonzero(keep)\n    if len(keep_idx) < MIN_TRIALS_PER_SESSION:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'only {len(keep_idx)} usable trials')\n\n    # ---------------- spikes ----------------\n    t0 = time.time()\n    br = BrainRegions()\n    neural_trials, region_acronyms, sorters = [], [], set()\n    for pid, pname in zip(session_info['pids'], session_info['probe_names']):\n        times, clu, clusters_df, collection = load_spiking_data(one, pid, eid, pname)\n        sorters.add(collection)\n        if len(clusters_df) == 0:\n            continue\n        beryl = np.asarray(br.acronym2acronym(clusters_df['acronym'].to_numpy(),\n                                              mapping='Beryl'), dtype=object)\n        # grey matter only (data paper) AND -- as in the reference `bin_spiking_data` --\n        # only clusters that actually emit spikes in this session\n        has_spikes = np.zeros(len(clusters_df), dtype=bool)\n        if clu.size:\n            has_spikes[np.unique(clu)] = True\n        sel = (~np.isin(beryl, NON_GREY_MATTER)) & has_spikes\n        if not sel.any():\n            continue\n        # remap kept clusters to rows 0..n-1\n        row_of = np.full(len(clusters_df), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n        row_of[np.flatnonzero(sel)] = np.arange(int(sel.sum()))\n        spike_sel = row_of[clu] >= 0\n        binned = bin_spikes(times[spike_sel], row_of[clu[spike_sel]],\n                            int(sel.sum()), interval_begs[keep_idx])\n        neural_trials.append(binned)\n        region_acronyms.append(beryl[sel])\n    if not neural_trials:\n        raise RuntimeError('no neurons passed curation')\n    neural = np.concatenate(neural_trials, axis=1)          # (n_trials, n_neurons, T)\n    regions = np.concatenate(region_acronyms)\n    if neural.shape[1] < MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'only {neural.shape[1]} well-isolated grey-matter neurons '\n                           f'(< {MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION})')\n    timings['spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    # ---------------- inputs ----------------\n    n_keep = len(keep_idx)\n    bin_centres = (TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE * (np.arange(N_BINS) + 0.5)).astype(np.float32)\n    inputs = np.empty((n_keep, 2, N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    inputs[:, 0, :] = bin_centres[None, :]\n    inputs[:, 1, :] = tnb_all[keep_idx].astype(np.float32)[:, None]\n\n    # ---------------- outputs ----------------\n    choice = trials_df['choice'].to_numpy()[keep_idx]        # +1 = left, -1 = right\n    choice_cls = (choice < 0).astype(np.int64)               # left -> 0, right -> 1\n    pleft = trials_df['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy()[keep_idx]\n    pleft_cls = np.full(len(pleft), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n    for val, cls in ((0.2, 0), (0.5, 1), (0.8, 2)):\n        pleft_cls[np.isclose(pleft, val)] = cls\n    if np.any(pleft_cls < 0):\n        bad = np.unique(pleft[pleft_cls < 0])\n        raise RuntimeError(f'unexpected probabilityLeft values {bad}')\n\n    ws = beh_vals['wheel_speed'][keep_idx]\n    wme = beh_vals['whisker_motion_energy'][keep_idx]\n    ws_edges = discretize_terciles(ws)\n    wme_edges = discretize_terciles(wme)\n    ws_cls = apply_terciles(ws, *ws_edges)\n    wme_cls = apply_terciles(wme, *wme_edges)\n\n    outputs = np.empty((n_keep, 4, N_BINS), dtype=np.int64)\n    outputs[:, 0, :] = choice_cls[:, None]\n    outputs[:, 1, :] = pleft_cls[:, None]\n    outputs[:, 2, :] = ws_cls\n    outputs[:, 3, :] = wme_cls\n\n    # ---------------- sanity checks ----------------\n    assert neural.shape[0] == n_keep == inputs.shape[0] == outputs.shape[0]\n    assert neural.shape[2] == N_BINS\n    assert len(regions) == neural.shape[1]\n    assert np.all(np.isfinite(neural)) and np.all(np.isfinite(inputs))\n    assert outputs.min() >= 0\n\n    if show_processing:\n        _plot_processing(plot_dir, eid, trials_df, keep_idx, interval_begs, traces,\n                         beh_vals, ws_edges, wme_edges, neural, inputs, outputs,\n                         regions, cam_used)\n\n    result = {\n        'eid': eid,\n        'subject': session_info['subject'],\n        'lab': session_info['lab'],\n        'date': str(session_info['date']),\n        'neural': [neural[k] for k in range(n_keep)],\n        'input': [inputs[k] for k in range(n_keep)],\n        'output': [outputs[k] for k in range(n_keep)],\n        'regions': regions,\n        'n_trials_raw': int(n_trials_raw),\n        'n_trials_kept': int(n_keep),\n        'n_neurons': int(neural.shape[1]),\n        'camera': cam_used,\n        'spike_sorter': sorted(sorters),\n        'wheel_speed_edges': ws_edges,\n        'whisker_me_edges': wme_edges,\n        'n_probes': len(session_info['pids']),\n        'timings': timings,\n        'runtime': time.time() - t_start,\n    }\n    return result\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef _plot_processing(plot_dir, eid, trials_df, keep_idx, interval_begs, traces, beh_vals,\n                     ws_edges, wme_edges, neural, inputs, outputs, regions, cam_used):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 2, beh_vals['wheel_speed'][tk], 'r.-', ms=4, lw=0.8,\n            label='interpolated at bin right edge')\n    ax.axhline(ws_edges[0], color='b', ls=':', label='tercile edges')\n ONE must be constructed as\n    35→`ONE(base_url='https://openalyx.internationalbrainlab.org', silent=True, cache_dir='/app/data/one_cache')`\n    36→**without** `password=` (supplying a password forces re-authentication over the network and\n    37→fails). With the staged auth token + `.rest` cache, ONE runs in `remote` mode but answers every\n    38→query from the on-disk REST cache. Pure `mode='local'` does **not** work because the release\n    39→parquet tables do not contain the dataset revisions that are actually staged on disk\n    40→(e.g. trials are staged under `alf/#2025-03-03#/`, which no table lists).\n    41→\n    42→---\n    43→\n    44→## Step 1: Reference Code Exploration\n    45→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    46→\n    47→`/app/code/code_zhang2025` is the Zhang et al. decoding repository. The data-preparation entry\n    48→point is `src/0_data_caching.py`, which calls helpers in `src/utils/ibl_data_utils.py`.\n    49→\n    50→### Key Functions Identified\n    51→| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n    52→|----------|------|-------|---------|\n    53→| `prepare_data(one, eid, bwm_df, params)` | `src/utils/ibl_data_utils.py` | LOADING | Top level per-session loader: resolves probes via `one.eid2pid`, loads spike sorting per probe, merges probes, loads trials + trial mask, loads continuous behaviours. |\n    54→| `load_spiking_data(one, pid, qc=None)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | LOADING/CURATION | `SpikeSortingLoader(...).load_spike_sorting()` + `merge_clusters(...).to_df()`. `qc=None` → all clusters; `qc=1` → only clusters with `label >= 1` (i.e. the \"well-isolated\"/good units), re-indexing `spikes['clusters']`. |\n    55→| `merge_probes(spikes_list, clusters_list)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | LOADING | Concatenates probes of one session into a single population (re-indexes cluster ids, sorts spikes by time). |\n    56→| `load_trials_and_mask(one, eid, min_rt=0.08, max_rt=2., nan_exclude='default', max_trial_len=..., exclude_nochoice=True)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | CURATION | Returns the full trials table plus a boolean keep-mask. Excludes NaNs in `stimOn_times, choice, feedback_times, probabilityLeft, firstMovement_times, feedbackType`; reaction time (`firstMovement_times - stimOn_times`) outside [0.08, 2.0] s; no-response trials (`choice == 0`). `prepare_data` additionally passes `max_trial_len=10.0` (excludes `feedback_times - goCue_times > 10 s`). |\n    57→| `list_brain_regions(neural_dict, **kwargs)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | PROCESSING | Maps each cluster's Allen `acronym` to the **Beryl** atlas mapping via `iblatlas.regions.BrainRegions.acronym2acronym(..., mapping='Beryl')`. |\n    58→| `select_brain_regions(...)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | CURATION | Index of clusters whose Beryl region is in the requested set (with `single_region=False` this is *all* clusters). |\n    59→| `bin_spiking_data(reg_clu_ids, neural_df, trials_df, **params)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | PROCESSING | Builds per-trial intervals `[align_time + t0, align_time + t1]`, then bins spikes into `binsize` bins → array `(n_trials, n_bins, n_clusters)`. Only clusters that emit ≥1 spike in the session appear as columns (`clusters_used_in_bins = np.unique(regclu)`). |\n    60→| `get_spike_data_per_interval(...)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | PROCESSING | Per-trial `bincount2D(times, clusters, xbin=binsize, xlim=[t_beg, t_end])`, truncated to `n_bins = ceil(interval_len/binsize)`. Bin index = `floor((t - t_beg)/binsize)`, spikes selected with `t_beg <= t < t_end`. |\n    61→| `load_target_behavior(one, eid, target)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | LOADING | `SessionLoader.load_wheel()` → `wheel-speed = abs(velocity)`; `SessionLoader.load_motion_energy(views=['left'/'right'])` → `whiskerMotionEnergy`. |\n    62→| `get_behavior_per_interval(...)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | PROCESSING | Cuts the continuous behaviour trace to each trial interval and **linearly interpolates** onto `np.linspace(t_beg + binsize, t_end, n_bins)` — i.e. the behaviour sample for bin *i* is taken at the **right edge** of the spike bin *i*. Marks a trial bad if the trace is absent, starts >1 binsize after `t_beg`, or ends >1 binsize before `t_end`. |\n    63→| `bin_behaviors(one, eid, behaviors, trials_df, ...)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | PROCESSING | Assembles per-trial scalars (`choice`, `probabilityLeft` as `block`, `reward`, signed `contrast`) and the time-varying behaviours. For `whisker-motion-energy` it uses the **left** camera, falling back to the **right** camera when the left is unavailable. |\n    64→| `align_spike_behavior(binned_spikes, binned_behaviors, beh_names, trials_mask)` | `ibl_data_utils.py` | CURATION | Deletes trials that are masked out by the trial mask or for which any behaviour is missing, so neural and behaviour arrays have identical trial counts. |\n    65→| `standardize_spike_data` / `SingleSessionDataset` | `src/utils/data_loader_utils.py` | PROCESSING | Model-side (not data-side) per-time-bin z-scoring of spike counts and `StandardScaler` for continuous behaviours; one-hot encoding of discrete targets. |\n    66→\n    67→### Reference caching parameters (`src/0_data_caching.py`)\n    68→```python\n    69→params = {'interval_len': 2, 'binsize': 0.02, 'single_region': False,\n    70→          'align_time': 'stimOn_times', 'time_window': (-.5, 1.5)}\n    71→beh_names = ['choice', 'reward', 'block', 'wheel-speed', 'whisker-motion-energy']\n    72→```\n    73→i.e. **align to `stimOn_times`, window −0.5 s to +1.5 s, 20 ms bins ⇒ T = 100 bins**. This is\n    74→exactly the alignment requested by the decoder task.\n    75→\n    76→### Notes on running the reference code as-is\n    77→- `load_spiking_data` calls `spike_loader.raw_electrophysiology(band='ap', stream=True).fs`,\n    78→  which needs network streaming of raw AP data; it is only used to report `sampling_freq` and is\n    79→  skipped here.\n    80→- `load_trials_and_mask` / `load_target_behavior` construct `SessionLoader(one, eid)`\n    81→  positionally; in the installed `brainbox` version `SessionLoader` is a dataclass and must be\n    82→  constructed with keywords (`SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)`). The conversion script passes an\n    83→  explicitly constructed `sess_loader` (the function already supports this) / calls the loader\n    84→  directly.\n    85→- The multiprocessing helpers (`get_spike_data_per_interval`, `get_behavior_per_interval`) spawn\n    86→  a `Pool` per session per signal. For 459 sessions this is very slow, so the conversion\n    87→  re-implements the *same arithmetic* vectorised (verified to match the reference function\n    88→  exactly — see Step 10).\n    89→\n    90→### Do cells need to be filtered based on quality?\n    91→Yes. `load_spiking_data` documents `qc=1` → \"use good clusters\" (`label >= 1`). The data paper\n    92→(methods.txt, \"Neurons and brain regions\") is explicit: neurons that fail any of the three RIGOR\n    93→single-unit metrics (amplitude > 50 µV, noise cut-off < 20 µV, refractory-period violation) are\n    94→excluded, leaving 75,708 \"well-isolated neurons\" out of 621,733 units. See Step 5 for the\n    95→decision taken here.\n    96→\n    97→(No imaging data is involved, so no ΔF/F computation is required.)\n    98→\n    99→---\n   100→\n   101→## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n   102→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   103→\n   104→### Data Structure\n   105→`/app/data/one_cache` is an ALyx/ONE cache laid out as\n   106→`<lab>/Subjects/<subject>/<date>/<number>/…`:\n   107→\n   108→```\n   109→alf/                                   session-level ALF files\n   110→  #2025-03-03#/_ibl_trials.table.pqt   trial table (revisioned!)\n   111→  _ibl_wheel.position.npy, _ibl_wheel.timestamps.npy\n   112→  #2025-05-29#/leftCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy\n   113→  #2025-05-31#/rightCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy\n   114→  #2023-04-20#/_ibl_{left,right}Camera.times.npy\n   115→  probe00/pykilosort/#2024-05-06#/{spikes,clusters,channels}.*.npy|pqt\n   116→  probe01/pykilosort/#2024-05-06#/…\n   117→raw_ephys_data/…                       (only .meta/.ch stubs staged)\n   118→```\n   119→Most datasets are staged under dataset *revisions*, so file access must go through ONE\n   120→(which resolves revisions), not through hard-coded paths.\n   121→\n   122→Release tables present: `Brainwidemap` (480 sessions / 76,563 datasets — the superset),\n   123→`2022_Q4_IBL_et_al_BWM` (354 sessions), `2025_Q3_IBL_et_al_BWM` (459 sessions, newer\n   124→lightning-pose / motion-energy revisions).\n   125→\n   126→The session/probe freeze used by the reference code is\n   127→`/app/code/code_zhang2025/data/bwm_release.csv`: **699 pids, 459 eids, 139 subjects** — exactly\n   128→the public BWM release.\n   129→\n   130→### Available variables (per session)\n   131→- `trials` table: `stimOn_times, stimOnTrigger_times, stimOff_times, goCue_times,\n   132→  goCueTrigger_times, response_times, feedback_times, feedbackType, rewardVolume, choice,\n   133→  contrastLeft, contrastRight, probabilityLeft, firstMovement_times, intervals_0/1,\n   134→  intervals_bpod_0/1, quiescencePeriod`\n   135→- `wheel`: `times, position, velocity, acceleration` (interpolated to 1 kHz by `SessionLoader`)\n   136→- `motion_energy`: `leftCamera` (60 Hz) / `rightCamera` (150 Hz) `whiskerMotionEnergy`\n   137→- spike sorting per probe: `spikes.times/clusters/amps/depths`, `clusters.*` (incl. the QC\n   138→  `label` ∈ {0, 1/3, 2/3, 1}), `channels.*` with Allen `acronym` per cluster.\n   139→\n   140→### Dataset Size (measured directly from the data files, restricted to the 459 BWM eids)\n   141→| Statistic | Value |\n   142→|-----------|-------|\n   143→| Units (all, total) | **621,733** |\n   144→| Units (all) / probe | 889.5 |\n   145→| Well-isolated units (`label >= 1`) total | **75,708** |\n   146→| Well-isolated units / probe | 108.3 |\n   147→| Probes (pids) | 699 |\n   148→| Subjects | **139** |\n   149→| Sessions | **459** |\n   150→| Sessions / subject | 3.30 (mean) |\n   151→| Trials (total, before curation) | **296,090** |\n
